# Encoder 1301, world 1: decision 12

[Gallery index](index.md)

This gallery preserves all 552 scheduled first narrator positions and their 552 literal-template controls in the fixed export order across 12 archive cases. No favorable examples were selected. These are repeated reporting opportunities, not 552 independent worlds.

`A_only` is a hybrid record: copied W11 fields with the previous node replaced by a source current estimate. Conditional candidates inherit that record and replace the query node with a source forecast. Ordinary and analytic sources also model attention effects. Joined records additionally supply native W.

Narrator text is raw first narration, not certified faithful or conscious. Literal templates are deterministic controls. Incomplete, missing, invalid, or blank text remains explicitly unavailable or blank according to the unchanged `pipeline._report_text` helper; no partial response is promoted into a complete text. The exact returned text is HTML-escaped inside `<pre>` blocks. No prose-auditor or reference-rater outcomes are used.

## 01. s1301_w01_t12_A_A_only_baseline

Source: `A`; view: `A_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `e7fedd62092e3ef015fcb9e52ea2cc9324bbe7e214aa86fcd7c87d9949907537`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible location distributions for red and blue at four addressed nodes on a 4x4 grid (rows down, columns right). Current estimates (view current) are: n0 — blue peak set {(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)}, red peak set {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)}; n1 — blue peak set {(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)}, red peak set {(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)}; n2 — blue peak {0,1}, red peak {0,2}; n3 — blue peak {1,3}, red peak {0,0}. Origins show some current entries were copied from a prior record or inherited history and therefore are not new estimates. Candidate conditional records (commands k0,k1,k2) are conditional command estimates, not executed events. For nodes n0, n2 and n3 the candidate peaks match the current peaks. For node n1 each command places blue at cell (1,2) and red at (0,0) but with different concentration (peak probabilities and entropies differ across k0, k1, k2).</pre>

Narrator text SHA-256: `7ac92d000c6e152e0e375c94e0e0d935f38c46cfa55b6e1e171c3aa85049bff5`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains probabilistic location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 on independent 4×4 local grids (rows increase downward, columns increase rightward). Origins: copied or inherited records noted in the packet are prior-record copies and not new current estimates; candidate command records (k0,k1,k2) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Current (view=current) summaries: n0 — blue mass spread over the top-left 2×2 cells; red mass over the top-right 2×2 cells. n1 — blue mass concentrated on the two rightmost columns of the top two rows (four-cell peak set); red mass concentrated on the top-left 2×2 cells. n2 — blue peaked at row 0, column 1; red peaked at row 0, column 2. n3 — blue peaked at row 1, column 3; red peaked at row 0, column 0. Candidate-command records modify mainly node n1: under k0–k2 blue shifts to a single-cell peak at [1,2] and red to [0,0] (k1/k2 show slightly different entropies and peak probabilities). Other candidate distributions match the current peaks shown above.</pre>

<pre>Panel p0, current estimates (represented_time 12, copied or inherited origins as supplied):
- n0: blue distribution peaks on the top-left 2×2 cells (each peak probability ≈0.180456); red peaks on the top-right 2×2 cells (each ≈0.249071).
- n1: blue peaks on the right 2×2 top rows (combined peak cells listed); red peaks on the left 2×2 top rows (each ≈0.249392).
- n2: blue peaks at cell [0,1] (peak probability ≈0.55043); red peaks at [0,2] (peak probability ≈0.999222).
- n3: blue peaks at [1,3] (≈0.99991); red peaks at [0,0] (≈0.984658).
Candidate records (commands k0,k1,k2, represented_time 13) are conditional estimates, not executed events. Most candidate distributions match current peaks for n0,n2,n3. ## 04. s1301_w01_t12_A_A_only_prospective_donor

Narrator text SHA-256: `8110f0c0059c71d3a38d446e4cd0db6b33ded1084ef2932a5c317a5454262eef`.

Narrator text:

## 08. s1301_w01_t12_A_joined_baseline

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `deb9aa6287049464be2096ac28751b4b34c793d83695f07a73e900219fc204dd`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains paired location distributions for blue and red roles on a 4×4 grid at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Current distributions (view=current) are shown separately from conditional command estimates (views with command k0/k1/k2). In panel p0 current peaks: n0 blue on the top-left 2×2, red on the top-right 2×2; n1 blue on the right half 2×2, red on the left half 2×2; n2 blue at (0,1), red at (0,2); n3 blue at (1,3), red at (0,0). Panel p1 current peaks match p0 for n0,n2,n3; n1 current is right-half blue/left-half red. Conditional candidate records change some node-n1 and node-n0 uncertainties (examples: p0/p1 candidate k0 includes a uniform candidate at p1:n1, while other k-commands concentrate mass to single cells). Origins in the packet indicate copied priors, completed records, history estimates, and native conditional records as labeled; conditional records are not executed events.</pre>

## 09. s1301_w01_t12_A_joined_current_donor

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_donor`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `693cda56afc801ac80ff6f05440883caa6be3e169b9899532dc3611d65e754de`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0,p1) give fallible 4x4 location distributions for blue and red at nodes n0–n3. Current records (command null) are shown along with conditional candidate records (k0,k1,k2). Copied prior or inherited origins are not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional, not executed events. Summary of current estimates: in both panels n0 blue is multimodal on the upper-left 2×2 and n0 red on the upper-right 2×2; n1 differs between panels—p0 current concentrates blue on the upper-right 2×2 and red on the upper-left 2×2, while p1 current shows the same pattern; n2 current blue peaks at cell (0,1) and red at (0,2); n3 current blue peaks at (1,3) and red at (0,0). Candidate records vary by command; many candidate branches reproduce the same peak sets as current for n0, n2 and n3, while some candidate records for n1 are uniform or concentrate on single cells depending on command.</pre>

## 10. s1301_w01_t12_A_joined_current_restored

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `2fa56116270b888b79eaee9391a97edb7bff0e564f979ff2920fe03675af81e8`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains paired 4x4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Panel p0 current distributions (represented_time 12 or completed at 12) show: n0 blue peaked on the upper-left 2x2, red on the upper-right 2x2; n1 blue peaked on the upper-right 2x2, red on the upper-left 2x2; n2 blue peaked at (0,1), red at (0,2); n3 blue peaked at (1,3), red at (0,0). Panel p0 also contains conditional candidate records (k0,k1,k2) at represented_time 13; these are conditional estimates, not executed events. For p0 n1 the conditional records all keep blue peak at (1,2) and red peak at (0,0) while peak probabilities differ across k0–k2. Panel p1 current distributions mirror p0 current for n0,n1,n2,n3 but come from completed history; its conditional candidates include a uniform alternative for n1 under k0 and more concentrated alternatives under k1 and k2. Copied-prior and inherited records in the packet are not new estimates; native conditional records are hypothetical branches, not executed events.</pre>

## 11. s1301_w01_t12_A_joined_prospective_donor

Source: `A`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `12a3f66537114dc8361328fe1ace588660e8b1c5e78de1649396ed9d833e02fc`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are not events that have already occurred. Current (view=current) summaries: in both panels n0’s blue mass is on the four top-left cells and red mass on the four top-right cells; n1’s current blue mass is on the four top-right cells while red is on the four top-left cells; n2’s blue peaks at cell (0,1) and red at (0,2); n3’s blue peaks at (1,3) and red at (0,0). Conditional candidate records (views with command k0/k1/k2) mostly preserve n0, n2 and n3 peaks but concentrate n1 in many command branches: under several commands n1’s blue distribution collapses to cell [1,2] and red to [0,0] with much higher single-cell peak probability than the current multi-cell pattern. Differences across k0/k1/k2 are therefore clearest at node n1; other node distributions remain similar across the supplied conditional records.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Current estimates (represented time 12–13) for each panel and node: in both panels n0’s blue distribution peaks across the top-left 2×2 cells and red across the top-right 2×2 cells. In both panels n2’s blue peak is at row 0, column 1 and its red peak at row 0, column 2. In both panels n3’s blue peak is at row 1, column 3 and red peak at row 0, column 0. Panel p0 current for n1 places blue mass on the right 2×2 (cells listed) and red on the left 2×2. Panel p1 current for n1 matches that same split. Candidate (k0,k1,k2) records are conditional estimates, not executed events; they differ primarily at node n1 across commands (p1: k0 uniform over all cells; k1 and k2 concentrate on right-side cells to varying degrees; p0 candidates vary concentration versus the current split). No physical or presence claims are made; peaks and described differences are properties of the supplied distributions only.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Current panels: p0 and p1 report paired fallible distributions for roles red and blue at nodes n0–n3 on a 4×4 grid. In panel p0 current (copied_prior_record, record_time 11 represented_time 12 for n0; other current entries include copied_prior_record or history_estimate with represented_time 12) blue peaks are n0:{(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)}, n1:{(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)}, n2:{(0,1)}, n3:{(1,3)}; red peaks are n0:{(0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3)}, n1:{(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)}, n2:{(0,2)}, n3:{(0,0)}. Panel p1 current uses archived snapshot origins (represented_time 12) and shows the same peak sets by node as p0. Candidate records (commands k0,k1,k2) are conditional command estimates or native conditional records (represented_time 13); they are not executed events. Peaks under those conditional records differ at node n1 across commands (examples: p0/p1 k0 includes blue uniform at p1 n1 for k0, while k1 and k2 concentrate blue peaks at (1,2) or (0,2) depending on the command). Copied prior records are noted as inherited, not new estimates.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains two panels (p0, p1). Current distributions: in both panels node n0’s blue mass is multimodal over the four upper-left cells and red mass is multimodal over the two rightmost columns of the top two rows; n1 differs between panels (p0 current: blue concentrated on the two right columns of the top two rows, red concentrated on the two left columns there; p1 current: same pattern as p0 for n1); n2’s blue peaks at (0,1) and red at (0,2) in all current records; n3’s blue peaks at (1,3) and red at (0,0) in all current records. Candidate command records (k0,k1,k2) change some node-n1 and node-n1-related peaks: some candidates place blue at a single cell (1,2) and red at (0,0) for n1, others leave the multimodal patterns; other nodes (n0,n2,n3) remain with the same peak cells across candidates. Copied or inherited origins and conditional-command origins are metadata; conditional-command entries are alternative candidate distributions, not executed events.</pre>

## 15. s1301_w01_t12_ordinary_A_only_baseline

Source: `ordinary`; view: `A_only`; variant: `baseline`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `eaa6731eba3e74f36646963493be9abd90131bb21c96e967ecb32ef68884f87f`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (represented_time 12) entries: n0 blue mass is peaked on the four upper-left cells [(0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1)]; n0 red peaks on the right two columns of the top two rows. n1 (current) has blue concentrated on the top-right 2×2 block and red concentrated on the top-left 2×2 block. n2 current has blue peaked at (0,1) and red peaked at (0,2). n3 current has blue peaked at (1,3) and red peaked at (0,0). Origins: current records for n0–n2 are copied/prior records and n3 is an inherited history_estimate; copied priors are not new estimates. Candidate distributions are command-conditioned estimates for commands k0, k1, k2 (feedback_through 11) and are conditional forecasts, not executed events. Across commands the main variation appears at n1: all three command-conditioned red distributions peak at (0,0) with near-certainty while blue shifts to (1,2) with peak probabilities varying by command (e.g., ~0.906, ~0.989, ~0.968). Other nodes’ candidate peaks match their current peaks in these records.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 current distributions (represented_time 12 for most nodes; node n3 represented_time 12 from history_estimate) are inherited copies and are not new measurements. Current: n0 — blue peaks in the four upper-left cells, red peaks in the four upper-right cells. n1 — blue peaks in the four upper-right cells, red peaks in the four upper-left cells. n2 — blue peak at (0,1), red peak at (0,2). n3 — blue peak at (1,3), red peak at (0,0). Candidate command records k0, k1, k2 are conditional estimates (feedback_through or command_estimate origins) and do not represent executed events. Across commands the n0, n2 and n3 candidate peaks match current. At n1 all candidate commands keep blue peaked at (1,2) and red at (0,0) but differ in concentration (peak probabilities and entropies supplied).</pre>

## 17. s1301_w01_t12_ordinary_A_only_current_restored

Source: `ordinary`; view: `A_only`; variant: `current_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `d682376730957eda2336856a6fbc4c7d74ec35db589cd348338d5a16015d62ed`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 contains fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current (represented time 12) summaries: n0 blue mass is split among the four top-left cells; n0 red peaks are the two top-right and two second-row right cells. At n1 current blue mass concentrates on the four right-column cells while current red concentrates on the four left-column cells (peaks swapped versus some conditionals). n2 current blue peaks at row 0, column 1 and n2 red peaks at row 0, column 2. n3 current blue peaks at row 1, column 3 and n3 red peaks at row 0, column 0. Candidate command records k0, k1 and k2 provide alternative conditional distributions for the same nodes: n0, n2 and n3 share the same peak sets across conditions; n1 shows conditional shifts where blue often peaks at cell [1,2] and red often peaks at [0,0], differing from the current panel-wide bilateral split at n1. Copied or conditional origins in the packet are metadata, not events or new executed measurements.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Panel p0 provides current and conditional (candidate) 4×4 discrete location distributions for roles red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Current estimates (represented time 12) show: n0 blue mass concentrated on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 0–1 and n0 red on the 2×2 block rows 0–1, cols 2–3; n1 blue concentrated on cols 2–3 rows 0–1 while n1 red is concentrated on the 2×2 block at rows 0–1, cols 0–1; n2 blue peaks at cell (0,1) and n2 red at (0,2); n3 blue peaks at (1,3) and n3 red at (0,0). Conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) are candidate estimates, not executed events; copied prior entries are inherited and not new estimates. Across the conditional records the primary differences appear at node n1 where candidate commands concentrate blue probability at cell (1,2) while red peaks at (0,0) under those command-conditioned estimates.</pre>

Narrator text SHA-256: `c3c13fb06de3e5da5b86bf6a5f40c4fce92f22a3ad061d098a1518703c8fbc72`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains two panels (p0 and p1). In each panel the current distributions (view &quot;current&quot;) are the reported baseline probability fields; where origins indicate copied prior records or completed records those current entries are not new estimates. Candidate records labeled k0, k1 and k2 are conditional command estimates (represented_time 13) and are not executed events. Panel p0 current: n0 — blue peaks on the top-left 2×2, red peaks on the top-right 2×2; n1 — blue peaks on the right 2×2, red peaks on the left 2×2; n2 — blue peak at (0,1), red peak at (0,2); n3 — blue peak at (1,3), red peak at (0,0). Panel p1 current: similar node-wise peaks (n1 and n0 show the same left/right 2×2 pattern; n2 and n3 match p0). Across conditional candidates some nodes change: for example node n1 has a uniform conditional in p1 k0, while other conditionals (k0/k1/k2 in both panels) concentrate blue at (1,2) or the right 2×2 and concentrate red at (0,0) with varying peak probabilities. Treat all listed probabilities and peaks as distributional data only.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions for &#x27;red&#x27; and &#x27;blue&#x27; over 4×4 local grids at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current panel p0 estimates: n0 — blue mass concentrated on the four top-left cells, red on the four top-right; n1 — blue on the four top-right, red on the four top-left; n2 — blue peak at (row0,col1), red at (0,2); n3 — blue peak at (1,3), red at (0,0). They differ at node n1 and at n1’s confidence across commands: k0 and k2 show a single-cell blue peak at (1,2) while other candidates and current records place blue mass on the top-right 2×2; other nodes’ candidate peaks match current peaks. Copied or inherited prior records are presented as inherited current entries (not new estimates). No claims beyond these distributional peaks are made.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet reports fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Current distributions (view = current) are given separately from conditional candidate distributions (commands k0,k1,k2). In panel p0 current: blue peaks concentrate on four cells in the upper-left 2×2 at n0; at n1 blue peaks are concentrated on the upper-right 2×2 while red peaks concentrate on the upper-left 2×2; at n2 blue peaks at (0,1) and red at (0,2); at n3 blue peaks at (1,3) and red at (0,0). Panel p1 current matches p0 for n0,n2,n3; at p1 n1 current blue peaks on the upper-right 2×2 and red on the upper-left 2×2. Conditional candidate records show alternative peak sets under commands k0–k2 for some nodes (noting k0 includes a uniform candidate at p1 n1). Copied or inherited origins and conditional-record semantics are preserved as labeled in the packet.</pre>

## 25. s1301_w01_t12_ordinary_joined_prospective_donor

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `c038c7141b58777423365c1a70350167bb28b5bcc4b581944c1dcbeeaa6e4509`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains two panels (p0, p1) of fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3. Origins and timestamps are in the record; copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records (k0/k1/k2) are conditional branches, not executed events. Current (view=current) summaries: in both panels n0 blue peaks are the four-cell set in the upper-left 2×2 and red peaks the upper-right 2×2; n1 (current) has blue concentrated on the two rightmost columns of the top two rows (four cells) and red on the corresponding left 2×2; n2 blue peaks at cell [0,1] and red at [0,2]; n3 blue peaks at [1,3] and red at [0,0]. Conditional command records differ at node n1 across commands: in several candidate records blue concentrates on [1,2] with very high peak probability and red concentrates on [0,0]; other conditionals include a uniform candidate for p1:k0. Panel p1 shows variants from p0 in some conditional branches and a slightly stronger concentration at n3 in its current record. No physical, presence or subjective claims are inferred; numbers are distributions only.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible 4x4 location distributions for red and blue roles at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Copied prior records are inherited, not new independent estimates; conditional command records (candidate views) are conditional hypothetical estimates, not executed events. Current (view=&quot;current&quot;) summaries: in both panels n0 blue peaks are the four upper-left 2x2 cells and red peaks the upper-right 2x2 cells. At n1 current the blue peaks are the upper-right 2x2 and red peaks are the upper-left 2x2 (panel p0 and p1). At n2 current blue peaks at (0,1) and red at (0,2). At n3 current blue peaks at (1,3) and red at (0,0). Conditional candidate records differ most at node n1: some command-conditioned candidates concentrate blue at (1,2) and red at (0,0), while one native conditional in p1 (k0) is uniform across all 16 cells. The propositions list gives the exact peak cell sets for every supplied distribution.</pre>

## 27. s1301_w01_t12_ordinary_joined_W_changed

Source: `ordinary`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_changed`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `7c5789cc714994868c2c6d731b6967766c4cf1086dd2b6c694f9695c6690bbcb`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions for blue and red roles on a 4×4 grid at four addressed nodes in two panels. Current distributions (view=current) are the primary reported estimates; some current entries are copied prior records (represented_time 12, record_time 11) or inherited counterfactual snapshots (panel p1, represented_time 12, archived_origin_time 13) and should not be treated as new observations. Conditional command records (views labeled candidate with command k0, k1 or k2) are alternative, conditional estimates, not executed events. In the current view: panel p0 and p1 share peak cells—n0 blue peaks at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1) and red at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3); n1 current swaps those peaks (blue at the right-half 0,2–1,3; red at the left-half 0,0–1,1); n2 current has blue at (0,1), red at (0,2); n3 current has blue at (1,3), red at (0,0). Conditional commands provide alternate peak assignments at some nodes (for example, at panel p0 node n1 candidate k0/k1/k2 place blue peak at (1,2) and red at (0,0)). Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional records are hypothetical alternatives.</pre>

Current (view=current) estimates: n0 — blue mass concentrated on the top-left 2×2 cells, red on the top-right 2×2; n1 — blue concentrated on the top-right 2×2, red on the top-left 2×2; n2 — blue peak at (0,1), red peak at (0,2); n3 — blue peak at (1,3), red peak at (0,0). Conditional command views (k0,k1,k2) show differences at node n1 in particular: k0 makes both roles uniform at n1; k1 flips blue to top-right and red to top-left; k2 places blue at cell (1,2) and red at (0,0). The prose summarizes distributional peaks and noted differences; no physical or presence inferences are made.</pre>

The packet contains only the supplied probability distributions and their derived peak sets; no physical facts or external inferences are asserted.</pre>

## 37. s1301_w01_t12_analytic_joined_current_donor

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `e0b9a6ef4bc8881b419dd9d03e120204b972cdb0bf8e425bff6ce47b71e421dc`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet gives fallible 4x4 location distributions for blue and red roles at nodes n0–n3 in two panels (p0, p1). Current-panel estimates (view=current) are listed per node and role: in both panels n0 blue peaks cover the 2x2 upper-left block and n0 red peaks cover the adjacent 2x2 block to the right; n1 and n3 show complementary 2x2 peak blocks (blue on the right block, red on the left block) in the current view; n2 current distributions place blue at cell (0,1) and red at (0,2) with the highest probabilities. Candidate command records (view=candidate, commands k0/k1/k2) present alternative conditional estimates rather than events. Across commands, node n1 shows the largest variation: some command variants are uniform over the grid, others shift peaks so blue favors right-side cells and red favors left-side cells, and one command concentrates nearly all mass on a single cell pair (different corners depending on panel and command). Panel p1’s current and candidate records for n3 include alternatives with very strong single-cell peaks (blue at (1,3), red at (0,0)) not present in p0 current. Origins and times in the packet indicate copied or inherited prior records and conditional estimates; these are data, not assertions of occurrence.</pre>

## 38. s1301_w01_t12_analytic_joined_current_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `current_restored`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `45ca8fb1f9629af1642e72400f4e9bb9e10a6ddb6b627daadd0a70460d84eb63`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions over 4×4 local grids for blue and red roles at nodes n0–n3 in panels p0 and p1. Current estimates (represented times 12–13) are reported per panel: p0 current peaks — n0: blue at the top-left 2×2, red at the top-right 2×2; n1: blue top-right 2×2, red top-left 2×2; n2: blue at cell [0,1], red at [0,2]; n3: blue at [1,3], red at [0,0]. Panel p1 current peaks match those cells; p1 origins indicate completed records. Candidate conditional records (commands k0,k1,k2) present alternative distributions (not executed events). Notable differences include for node n1: k0 gives uniform peaks (all 16 cells) in p0/p1; k1 swaps quadrant preferences between roles; k2 places blue peak at [1,2] and red at [0,0]. Copied prior records are not new estimates; conditional command records are conditional alternatives only.</pre>

## 39. s1301_w01_t12_analytic_joined_prospective_donor

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_donor`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `1e44086758facb51cf3634601170d5ebd3a15144630ab3895e26993e51456fa6`.

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0, p1) contain fallible 4×4 location distributions for red and blue at nodes n0–n3. Origins vary (copied prior records or completed_record entries at times 11–12 for inherited current records; candidate k* entries are conditional estimates, not executed events). Current (view=current) summaries: p0 and p1 match and show n0 blue peaked on the 2×2 top-left cells and red peaked on the 2×2 top-right; n1 blue peaked on the 2×2 top-right and red on the 2×2 top-left; n2 blue peaked at (0,1) and red at (0,2); n3 blue peaked at (1,3) and red at (0,0). Conditional command records (view=candidate) vary by command: for example node n1 across k0/k1/k2 alternates a concentrated single-cell peak (k0), a 2×2 peak (k1), or a uniform 16-cell peak (k2). Copied prior records are not new estimates; candidate records are conditional forecasts, not events that have occurred.</pre>

## 40. s1301_w01_t12_analytic_joined_prospective_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `prospective_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `7e427411489515e6f5d4cc0d421ee24fabeaa9d84887e65fbef3122d150db770`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains paired location distributions (red and blue) over a 4×4 grid at four addressed nodes (n0–n3) in two panels (p0, p1). Copied prior records are not new estimates, and conditional command records are conditional possibilities, not events that have occurred. Current view (command null) gives these modal sets per node and panel: p0 current — n0: blue peaks at the top-left 2×2, red peaks at the top-right 2×2; n1: blue top-right 2×2, red top-left 2×2; n2: blue at cell (0,1), red at (0,2); n3: blue at (1,3), red at (0,0). p1 current — n0 same as p0 n0; n1 same quadrant pairing as p0 current; n2 and n3 match p0. Conditional command records (k0,k1,k2) change some node n1 distributions: k0 in both panels makes n1 uniform; k1 swaps quadrant assignments in some panels; k2 can concentrate blue on cell (1,2) and red on (0,0). No physical, presence or subjective claims are made; numerical peaks and uncertainty describe only the supplied distributions.</pre>

Narrator text:

<pre>Two panels (p0 and p1) provide fallible 4×4 location distributions for blue and red at nodes n0–n3. Current view (inherited/copied records, represented time 12–13) shows identical current peaks in both panels: n0 — blue peaks at (0,0),(0,1),(1,0),(1,1); red peaks at (0,2),(0,3),(1,2),(1,3). n1 — blue peaks at the top-right four cells; red peaks at the top-left four cells. n2 — blue peak at (0,1); red peak at (0,2). n3 — blue peak at (1,3); red peak at (0,0). Candidate command records (k0,k1,k2) are conditional/hypothetical, not executed events. Most nodes (n0,n2,n3) keep the same peaks across commands; n1 differs across commands: under k0 it is uniform; under k1 it swaps blue/red quadrants; under k2 it concentrates to single-cell peaks. Copied prior records are labeled inherited and are not new independent estimates.</pre>

## 42. s1301_w01_t12_analytic_joined_W_restored

Source: `analytic`; view: `joined`; variant: `W_restored`.

Narrator transport complete: `true`; availability: `retained_first_outcome`; API status: `completed`.

Narrator text SHA-256: `852831aedfc9ea81743f67aca190aad4b958c3d70b6e981e5bd9a17d7f236bd8`.

Narrator text:

<pre>This packet contains fallible location distributions for red and blue roles on 4×4 local grids in two panels (p0, p1). Origins include copied_prior_record (record_time 11, represented_time 12), completed_record (record_time 12), and command or native_conditional records represented_time 13; copied priors are not new estimates and conditional command records are conditional, not executed events. Current (view=current) estimates in both panels: n0 blue peaks on the upper-left 2×2 block and red peaks on the upper-right 2×2 block; n1 blue peaks on the upper-right 2×2 and red on the upper-left 2×2; n2 blue peaks at (0,1) and red at (0,2); n3 blue peaks at (1,3) and red at (0,0). Candidate command records show differences: some commands (k0) match the current patterns, k1 can produce uniform distributions at n1, and k2 can shift peaks (for example n1 blue→(1,2) and red→(0,0)). No physical or presence claims are made; listed peaks report the modal cell(s) of each supplied distribution.</pre>
